I... I guess other people think it's strange. Um... like, I'll see certain stains on my materials, or... the way a seam is sewn... and I just know it's a warning. Or an instruction about my work. It happens with phrases in gallery emails, too. I just know they're meant specifically for me.

I... I don't know how to explain it so you'll understand. It just... it feels unique to me, I guess. Like no one else would see it.

Yeah. I do. 

Like I said... they try to explain it away. They say it's just coffee, or a typo in an email from the gallery. But... it doesn't change anything for me. I just... I still know it's a warning. Or an instruction. It's really hard to just let it go, even when they tell me it's nothing. I just can't.
